\section{Some Lemmas}\label{somelemmas}
%\renewcommand{\theassumption}{A\arabic{assumption}}
\setcounter{assumption}{0}


In order to establish the law of large numbers for the quadratic form of $\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathbb{V}$,
we need to study the statistical properties of the quadratic form of two random variables formed by the accumulation of infinite historical errors. Define ${P_t}$, ${Q_t}$ as sequences of $N_t\times N_{t-1}$ matrices, and define their cumulative products as $P_{t}^{(h)}\equiv P_{t}P_{t-1}\cdots P_{t-h+1}$ for $h=1,2,\cdots$, $P_{t}^{(0)}=I_{N_t}$ and $Q_{t}^{(h)}\equiv Q_{t}Q_{t-1}\cdots Q_{t-h+1}$ for $h=1,2,\cdots$, $Q_{t}^{(0)}=I_{N_t}$ respectively.
Then denote two different accumulations of infinite historical errors as $$L_t=\sum_{h=0}^\infty P_{th}V_{t-h},\quad R_t=\sum_{h=0}^\infty Q_{th}V_{t-h},$$ 
where $P_{th}\equiv B_{1t}P_{t}^{(h)}$, $Q_{th}\equiv B_{2t}Q_{t}^{(h)}$, and $B_{1t}$ and $B_{2t}$ are $N_t\times N_t$ matrices. Define their respective lagged versions as
$$L_t^\ell=B_{1t}^\ell L_{t-1}, \quad R_t^\ell=B_{2t}^\ell R_{t-1}, $$ 
where $B_{1t}^\ell$ and $B_{2t}^\ell$ are $N_t\times N_{t-1}$ matrices.
Denote their respective stacked versions as $\mathbb{L}\equiv(L_1',L_2',\cdots,$ $L_T')'$, $\mathbb{L}^\ell\equiv(L_1^{\ell\prime},L_2^{\ell\prime},\cdots,L_T^{\ell\prime})'$, and similarly, $\mathbb{R}$ and $\mathbb{R}^\ell$ follow the same form.
%Moreover, define their respective lagged stacked version as $\mathbb{L}^\ell\equiv(L_0',L_2',\cdots,D_{T-1}'L_{T-1}')'$ and $\mathbb{R}^\ell $.
Denote the block diagonal matrices $\mathbb{B}_1=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{11},$ $B_{12},\cdots,B_{1T})$ and $\mathbb{B}_2=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{21},B_{22},\cdots,B_{2T})$. Denote a nonstochastic $n$-dimensional vector as $\mathbb{C}=(C_1',C_2',\cdots,C_T')'$.
The following assumptions will be used in some of the subsequent lemmas:

\begin{assumption}\label{asp_A2}
$\{B_{1t}\}$, $\{B_{2t}\}$, $\{B_{1t}^\ell\}$, $\{B_{2t}^\ell\}$, $\{P_t^{(h)}\}$ and $\{Q_t^{(h)}\}$ are nonstochastic matrix sequences such that $B_{1t}$, $B_{2t}$, $B_{1t}^\ell$, $B_{2t}^\ell$, $\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(P_t^{(h)}D_{t-h})$, $\sum_{h=t}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)})$,  $\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(Q_t^{(h)}D_{t-h})$, $\sum_{h=t}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(D_h'Q_h^{(h-t)})$ are all UB uniformly for $t$. Also, $\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{jt}D_t)\|_\infty<\infty$ and $\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{jt}^\ell D_{t-1})\|_\infty<\infty$, for $j=1,2$.
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}\label{asp_C}
$C_t$ is an arbitrary nonstochastic $N_t$-dimensional vector, and elements in $C_t$ are uniformly bounded for $t$ and $N_t$.
\end{assumption}


\begin{lemma}\label{lm_EPQ}
Under Assumption 2, for $t\geq s$, 
$$E(L_tR_s')=\sigma_0^2\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'\right),$$
and
$$E(L_t'D_tD_s'R_s)=\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty D_t'P_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'D_s\right).$$ 
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Since $V_t$'s are independent across $t$,
\begin{align*}
E(L_tR_s')&=E\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1} P_{th}V_{t-h}+\sum_{g=0}^{\infty} P_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)'\right]\\
&=E\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty} P_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)'\right]=\sigma_0^2\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'\right).
\end{align*}
Additionally, 
\begin{align*}
E(L_t'D_tD_s'R_s)=&E(R_s'D_sD_t'L_t)=\mathrm{tr}\left[D_t'E(L_tR_s')D_s\right]=\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty D_t'P_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'D_s\right)
%=\sigma_0^2\sum_{g=0}^\infty\mathrm{tr}\left(Q_{sg}'D_sD_t'P_{t,t-s+g}\right).
\end{align*}
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_quadratic form}
Under Assumption 2, for any nonstochastic $B_1$ of size $N_t\times N_s$, $B_2$ of size $N_g\times N_h$, we have
\begin{align*}
E\left[(V_{t}^{\prime}B_{1}V_{s})(V_{g}^{\prime}B_{2}V_{h})\right]&=\left\{\begin{array}{ll}(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_t} B_{1,ii}B_{2,ii}&\mathrm{for}\ t=s=g=h\\
+\sigma_0^4(\mathrm{tr}B_{1}\times \mathrm{tr}B_{2}+\mathrm{tr}B_{1}B_{2}+\mathrm{tr}B_{1}B_{2}^{\prime})\\
\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}B_{1}\times \mathrm{tr}B_{2}&\mathrm{for}\ t=s\neq g=h\\
\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}(B_{1}B_{2}^{\prime})&\mathrm{for}\ t=g\neq s=h\\
\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}(B_{1}B_{2})&\mathrm{for}\  t=h\neq s=g\\
0&\rm{otherwise}
\end{array}\right..
\end{align*}
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
This lemma is an extension of Lemma 3 in Yu et al. (2008), which assumes that both $B_1$ and $B_2$ are square matrices of the same size. 
Denote the $i$-th element of $V_t$ as $V_{t,i}$. Then
\begin{align*}
E\left[(V_{t}^{\prime}B_{1}V_{s})(V_{g}^{\prime}B_{2}V_{h})\right]&=E\left[\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}V_{t,i}B_{1,ij}V_{s,j}\Bigg)\Bigg(\sum_{k=1}^{N_g}\sum_{l=1}^{N_h}V_{g,k}B_{2,kl}V_{h,l}\Bigg)\right]\\
&=E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{g}}\sum_{l=1}^{N_h}B_{1,ij}B_{2,kl}V_{t,i}V_{s,j}V_{g,k}V_{h,l}\right]
\end{align*}
Since $V_{t,i}$'s are i.i.d across $t$ and $i$, the equation above will not equal to $0$ only when $t=s=g=h$, $t=s\neq g=h$, $t=g\neq s=h$ and $t=h\neq s=g$. For $t=s=g=h$, our conclusion is exactly the same as the corresponding case of Lemma 3 in Yu et al. (2008). Therefore, there is no need for proof. When $t=s\neq g=h$,
%For $t=s=g=h$,
\begin{align*}
E\left[(V_{t}^{\prime}B_{1}V_{s})(V_{g}^{\prime}B_{2}V_{h})\right]=&E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_t}\sum_{k=1}^{N_g}\sum_{l=1}^{N_g}B_{1,ij}B_{2,kl}V_{t,i}V_{t,j}V_{g,k}V_{g,l}\right]\\
=&E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{k=1}^{N_g}B_{1,ii}B_{2,kk}V_{t,i}^2V_{g,k}^2\right]\\
=&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}B_{1}\times \mathrm{tr}B_{2}.
\end{align*}
When $t=g\neq s=h$,
\begin{align*}
E\left[(V_{t}^{\prime}B_{1}V_{s})(V_{g}^{\prime}B_{2}V_{h})\right]=&E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\sum_{k=1}^{N_t}\sum_{l=1}^{N_s}B_{1,ij}B_{2,kl}V_{t,i}V_{s,j}V_{t,k}V_{s,l}\right]\\
=&E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}B_{1,ij}B_{2,ij}V_{t,i}^2V_{s,j}^2\right]\\
=&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}(B_{1}B_2').
\end{align*}
When $t=h\neq s=g$,
\begin{align*}
E\left[(V_{t}^{\prime}B_{1}V_{s})(V_{g}^{\prime}B_{2}V_{h})\right]=&E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\sum_{k=1}^{N_s}\sum_{l=1}^{N_t}B_{1,ij}B_{2,kl}V_{t,i}V_{s,j}V_{s,k}V_{t,l}\right]\\
=&E\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}B_{1,ij}B_{2,ji}V_{t,i}^2V_{s,j}^2\right]\\
=&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}(B_{1}B_2).
\end{align*}
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_CovPQ}
Under Assumption 2, for $t\geq s$, 
\begin{align*}
&\mathrm{Cov}(L_t'R_t,L_s'R_s)\\
=&\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}(P_{t,t-s+g}'Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}(P_{sg}'Q_{sg})_{ii}\right]+\\
&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}'\right)\right].
\end{align*}
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Using Lemma \ref{lm_quadratic form}, we have
\begin{align*}
E(L_{t}^{\prime}R_{t}L_{s}^{\prime}R_{s}) 
=&E\left\lbrack\left(\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}P_{th}V_{t-h}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}Q_{th}V_{t-h}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)\right\rbrack\\  
=&E\left\lbrack\left(\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1}P_{th}V_{t-h}+\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1}Q_{th}V_{t-h}+\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)\right.\\  
&\left.\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)\right\rbrack\\  
=&E\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1}P_{th}V_{t-h}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1}Q_{th}V_{t-h}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)\right]+\\  
&E\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)\right]\\
\equiv&E_1+E_2,
\end{align*}
%We denote the first expectation of the second-to-last equation as $E_1$ and the second as $E_2$.
where the third equality holds because $V_t$'s are independent across $t$.
Then we have
\begin{align*}
E_1&=E\left\lbrack\left(\sum_{h_1=0}^{t-s-1}P_{th}V_{t-h}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{h_2=0}^{t-s-1}Q_{th}V_{t-h}\right)\left(\sum_{g_1=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g_2=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)\right\rbrack\\
&=E\left\lbrack\left(\sum_{h_1=0}^{t-s-1}\sum_{h_2=0}^{t-s-1}V_{t-h_1}^{\prime}P_{th_1}^{\prime}Q_{th_2}V_{t-h_2}\right)\left(\sum_{g_1=0}^{\infty}\sum_{g_2=0}^{\infty}V_{s-g_1}^{\prime}P_{sg_1}^{\prime}Q_{sg_2}V_{s-g_2}\right)\right\rbrack\\
&=\sigma_0^4\left\lbrack\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{th}^{\prime}Q_{th}\right)\right\rbrack\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)\right\rbrack,
\end{align*}
where the last equality holds due to the second case in Lemma \ref{lm_quadratic form}. 

\begin{align*}
E_{2}&=E\left\lbrack\left(\sum_{g_1=0}^{\infty}P_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g_2=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}V_{s-g}\right)\left(\sum_{g_3=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)^{\prime}\left(\sum_{g_4=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}V_{s-g}\right)\right\rbrack\\
&=E\left\lbrack\sum_{g_1=0}^{\infty}\sum_{g_2=0}^{\infty}\sum_{g_3=0}^{\infty}\sum_{g_4=0}^{\infty}\left(V_{s-g_1}^{\prime}P_{t,t-s+g_1}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g_2}V_{s-g_2}\right)\left(V_{s-g_3}^{\prime}P_{sg_3}^{\prime}Q_{sg_4}V_{s-g_4}\right)_{}\right\rbrack\\
&\equiv E_{21}+E_{22}+E_{23}+E_{24},
\end{align*}
where $E_{21}$, $E_{22}$, $E_{23}$ and $E_{24}$ are the four nonzero cases in Lemma \ref{lm_quadratic form}. Specifically, $E_{21}$ corresponds to the case where $g_1 = g_2 = g_3 = g_4$, so
\begin{align*}
E_{21}=&\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\Bigg[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg})_{ii}+\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)\\
&+\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)+\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}^{\prime}P_{sg}\right)\Bigg],
\end{align*}
$E_{22}$ corresponds to the case where $g_1 = g_2 \neq g_3 = g_4$,
\begin{align*}
E_{22}=\sigma_0^4\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}\right)\right\rbrack\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)\right\rbrack-\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right).
\end{align*}
$E_{23}$ corresponds to the case where  $g_1 = g_3 \neq g_2 = g_4$,
\begin{align*}
E_{23}&=\sigma_0^4\left\lbrack\sum_{g_1=0}^{\infty}\sum_{g_2=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g_1}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g_2}Q_{sg_2}^{\prime}P_{sg_1}\right)\right\rbrack-\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}^{\prime}P_{sg}\right)\\
&=\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left\lbrack \left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}^{\prime}\right)\right\rbrack-\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}^{\prime}P_{sg}\right).
\end{align*}
In addition, $E_{24}$ corresponds to the case where  $g_1 = g_4 \neq g_2 = g_3$,
\begin{align*}
E_{24}&=\sigma_0^4\left[\sum_{g_1=0}^{\infty}\sum_{g_2=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}(P_{t,t-s+g_1}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g_2}P_{sg_2}^{\prime}Q_{sg_1})\right]-\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg})\\
&=\sigma_0^4\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}^{\prime}\right)\right]-\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}).
\end{align*}
Therefore, we have
\begin{align*}
&\mathrm{Cov}(L_t'R_t,L_s'R_s)=E(L_t'R_tL_s'R_s)-E(L_t'R_t)E(L_s'R_s)\\
=&E_1+E_{21}+E_{22}+E_{23}+E_{24}-E(L_t'R_t)E(L_s'R_s)\\
=&\sigma_0^4\left\lbrack\sum_{h=0}^{t-s-1}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{th}^{\prime}Q_{th}\right)\right\rbrack\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)\right\rbrack+\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg})_{ii}\right]+\\
&\sigma_0^4\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}Q_{t,t-s+g}\right)\right\rbrack\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)\right\rbrack+\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left\lbrack \left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}^{\prime}\right)\right\rbrack+\\
&\sigma_0^4\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}^{\prime}\right)\right]-\sigma_0^4\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{tg}^{\prime}Q_{tg}\right)\right\rbrack\left\lbrack\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{sg}^{\prime}Q_{sg}\right)\right\rbrack\\
=&\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}(P_{t,t-s+g}'Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}(P_{sg}'Q_{sg})_{ii}\right]+\\
&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}'\right)\right],
\end{align*}
where $E(L_t'R_t)$ and $E(L_s'R_s)$ are calculated according to Lemma \ref{lm_EPQ}.
\end{proof}


\begin{lemma}\label{lm33}
Denote $B_t\equiv B_{1t}B_{2t}$ and $B_t^\ell\equiv B_{1t}^\ell B_{2,t-1}$. Under Assumption \ref{asp_A2},
%Based on the previous assumptions about $\{P_t^{(h)}\}$, $\{Q_t^{(h)}\}$, as well as $\{B_{1t}\}$ and $\{B_{2t}\}$,
%for any sequence of non-random matrix sequences $\{B_{t}\}$ of size $N_t\times N_t$ that is UB uniformly for $t$, and $\|B_{\sup}\|_\infty<\infty$, where $B_{\sup,ij}\equiv\sup_{h}|(D_hB_{h}D_h')_{ij}|$. 
the following facts hold:
%under their respective assumptions:
\begin{enumerate}[(1)]
\item $\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_tD_t)\|_{\infty}<\infty$ and $\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_t^\ell D_{t-1})\|_{\infty}<\infty$.
\item $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_hP_h^{(h-t)}\right)$ and $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(B_t^\ell P_{t-1}^{(h)}D_{t-1-h}\right)$ are UB uniformly for $t$.%只假设了负无穷到T的序列UB
\item  $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_h^{(h-t)\prime}B_{2h}'B_{1h}P_h^{(h-t)}\right)$ and $\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{t}^\ell P_{t-1}^{(h)}P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}\right)$ are UB uniformly for $t$.
\item $\sum_{h=0}^\infty \mathrm{abs}(P_t^{(h)})\mathrm{abs}(Q_t^{(h)\prime})$ are UB uniformly for $t$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
\textbf{Proof of (1):}
According to the definition of $\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_tD_t)$, we have
\begin{align*}
\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_tD_t)\|_{\infty}&=\sup_{1\leq i\leq N}\sum_{j=1}^N\sup_{t}\left|(D_t'B_tD_t)_{ij}\right|\\
&\leq\sup_{1\leq i\leq N}\sum_{j=1}^N\sup_{t}\sum_{k=1}^N\left|(D_t'B_{1t}D_t)_{ik}\right|\left|(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)_{kj}\right|\\
&\leq\sup_{1\leq i\leq N}\sum_{j=1}^N\sum_{k=1}^N\left(\sup_{t}\left|(D_t'B_{1t}D_t)_{ik}\right|\right)\left(\sup_{t}\left|(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)_{kj}\right|\right)\\
&\leq\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{1t}D_t)\|_\infty\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\|_\infty\\
&<\infty.
\end{align*}
Similarly, we can prove $\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_t^\ell D_{t-1})\|_{\infty}<\infty$.

\textbf{Proof of (2):}

Since we assume that $B_{1t}$ and $B_{2t}$,  are UB uniformly for $t$, $B_t$ is UB because $\|B_t\|_p\leq\|B_{1t}\|_p\|B_{2t}\|_p$ holds for both $p=1$ and $p=\infty$.
Moreover, $D_t'B_tD_t$ is also UB uniformly for $t$, because $D_t'B_tD_t$ only adds some zero rows and columns to $B_t$, which means that there exists a constant $c_B>0$, such that
$$\sup_{N,T}\sup_{t\leq T}\max\left(\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{1},\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{\infty},\left\|D_t'B_tD_t\right\|_{1},\left\|D_t'B_tD_t\right\|_{\infty}\right)\leq c_B.$$
Thus, we have
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_hP_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{1}&\leq\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}(D_h'B_hD_h)\mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{1}\\
&=\sup_{1\leq j\leq N}\sum_{i=1}^N\sum_{h=t}^\infty\sum_{k=1}^N\left|\left(D_h'B_hD_h\right)_{ik}\right|\left|\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)_{kj}\right|\\
&\leq\sup_{1\leq j\leq N}\sum_{h=t}^\infty\sum_{k=1}^N\left\|D_h'B_hD_h\right\|_1\left|\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)_{kj}\right|\\
&\leq c_{B}\sup_{1\leq j\leq N}\sum_{k=1}^N\sum_{h=t}^\infty\left|\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)_{kj}\right|\\
&=c_B\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{1}\\
&<\infty,
\end{align*}
and
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_hP_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{\infty}&\leq\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}(D_h'B_hD_h)\mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\\
&=\sup_{1\leq i\leq N}\sum_{j=1}^{N_t}\sum_{h=t}^\infty\sum_{k=1}^N\left|\left(D_h'B_hD_h\right)_{ik}\right|\left|\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)_{kj}\right|\\
&\leq\sup_{1\leq i\leq N}\sum_{h=t}^\infty\sum_{k=1}^N\sup_{h}\left|\left(D_h'B_hD_h\right)_{ik}\right|\sum_{j=1}^{N_t}\left|\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)_{kj}\right|\\
&\leq\sup_{1\leq i\leq N}\sum_{k=1}^N\sup_{h}\left|\left(D_h'B_hD_h\right)_{ik}\right|\sum_{j=1}^{N_t}\sum_{h=t}^\infty\left|\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)_{kj}\right|\\
&\leq\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{1t}D_t)\|_{\infty}\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\\
&<\infty.
\end{align*}
Therefore, $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_hP_h^{(h-t)}\right)$ is UB uniformly for $t$. Applying the same method, we can prove that $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(B_t^\ell P_{t-1}^{(h)}D_{t-1-h}\right)$ is UB uniformly for $t$.

\textbf{Proof of (3):}

According to (2), it is obvious that $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_{1h}P_h^{(h-t)}\right)$ and $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_{2h}Q_h^{(h-t)}\right)$ are also UB uniformly for $t$. Hence,
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_h^{(h-t)\prime}B_{2h}'B_{1h}P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_p&\leq\left\|\left[\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_h^{(h-t)\prime}B_{2h}'D_h\right)\right]\left[\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_{1h}P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right]\right\|_p\\
&\leq\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_h^{(h-t)\prime}B_{2h}'D_h\right)\right\|_p\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_h'B_{1h}P_h^{(h-t)}\right)\right\|_p\\
&<\infty.
\end{align*}
The above inequality holds for both $p=1$ and $p=\infty$. Thus $\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_h^{(h-t)\prime}B_{2h}'B_{1h}P_h^{(h-t)}\right)$ is also UB uniformly for $t$. Similarly, we can prove that $\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{t}^\ell P_{t-1}^{(h)}P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}\right)$ is UB uniformly for $t$.

\textbf{Proof of (4):}

Since $\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(P_t^{(h)}D_{t-h})$ and $\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(Q_t^{(h)}D_{t-h})$ are also UB uniformly for $t$, we similarly have
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(P_t^{(h)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(Q_t^{(h)\prime}\right)\right\|_p&\leq\left\|\left[\sum_{h=t}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(P_t^{(h)}D_{t-h}\right)\right]\left[\sum_{h=t}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_t^{(h)}D_{t-h}\right)'\right]\right\|_p\\
&\leq\left\|\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(P_t^{(h)}D_{t-h}\right)\right\|_p\left\|\sum_{h=0}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(Q_t^{(h)}D_{t-h}\right)'\right\|_p\\
&<\infty.
\end{align*}

%the proof method of (4) is identical to that of (3).
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_OnT}
Under Assumptions 2, 7 and \ref{asp_A2},
$$\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t'R_t\right)=\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T \mathrm{Cov}(L_t'R_t,L_s'R_s)=O(NT)$$
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
\begin{align}
\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t'R_t\right)=&\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{Cov}(L_t'R_t,L_t'R_t)+2\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{Cov}(L_t'R_t,L_s'R_s)\nonumber\\
\leq&2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathrm{Cov}(L_t'R_t,L_s'R_s)\nonumber\\
=&2\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'\right)\right]+\nonumber\\
&2\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}'\right)\right]\label{eq_Var}+\\
&2\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}(P_{t,t-s+g}'Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}(P_{sg}'Q_{sg})_{ii}\right].\nonumber
\end{align}
The last equality holds from Lemma \ref{lm_CovPQ}.
Denote $\mathcal{A}_{t,PP}\equiv\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_t^{(g)} P_t^{(g)\prime}$ and $\mathcal{A}_{t,QQ}\equiv\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_t^{(g)} Q_t^{(g)\prime}$, and they are UB from Lemma \ref{lm33}. We can derive the order of the first term of Equation (\ref{eq_Var}) as
\begin{align*}
&\left|2\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}Q_{sg}'\right)\right]\right|\\
=&\left|2\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathrm{tr}\left[B_{1s}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}P_{s}^{(g)}P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)P_{t}^{(t-s)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{(t-s)}\left(\sum_{g=0}^{\infty}Q_{s}^{(g)}Q_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)B_{2s}^{\prime}\right]\right|\\
=&\left|2\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathrm{tr}\left[B_{1s}\mathcal{A}_{s,PP}P_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)}\mathcal{A}_{s,QQ}B_{2s}^{\prime}\right]\right|\\
=&\left|2\sigma_0^4\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t=s}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[P_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)}\mathcal{A}_{s,QQ}B_{2s}^{\prime}B_{1s}\mathcal{A}_{s,PP}\right]\right|\\
\leq&2\sigma_0^4\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\sum_{t=s}^{T}\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(\mathcal{A}_{s,QQ}B_{2s}^{\prime}B_{1s}\mathcal{A}_{s,PP}\right)\right]\\
\leq&2\sigma_0^4\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\sum_{t=s}^{\infty}\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(\mathcal{A}_{s,QQ}B_{2s}^{\prime}B_{1s}\mathcal{A}_{s,PP}\right)\right]\\
=&O(NT).
\end{align*}
The first equality holds because $P_{th}=B_{1t}P_t^{(h)}$ and $P_t^{(t-s+g)}=P_t^{(t-s)}P_s^{(g)}$.
By (3) in Lemma \ref{lm33}, we know that $\sum_{t=s}^{\infty}\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{\left(t-s\right)}\right)$ is UB, then the term inside the trace operator of the second-to-last equation is an $N_s \times N_s$ UB matrix, so the last equality holds. Since $P_{th}$ and $Q_{th}$ are arbitrary matrices, under the same assumptions, we can similarly derive the order of the second term in equation (\ref{eq_Var}) as
$$2\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{sg}P_{t,t-s+g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty Q_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}'\right)\right]=O(NT).$$
Since $\sum_{g=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}(P_{sg}'Q_{sg})$ is UB by Lemma \ref{lm33}, there exists a finite constant $c_{PQ}$ such that $$\sup_{N}\sup_{s}\sup_{i=1,\cdots,N_s}\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left|(P_{sg}'Q_{sg})_{ii}\right|\leq c_{PQ},$$
therefore we have
\begin{align*}
&\left|\sum_{g=0}^\infty\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}(P_{t,t-s+g}'Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}(P_{sg}'Q_{sg})_{ii}\right|\\
\leq&c_{PQ}\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\sum_{g=0}^\infty\sum_{i=1}^{N_{s-g}}\left|(P_{t,t-s+g}'Q_{t,t-s+g})_{ii}\right|\\
\leq&c_{PQ}\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\sum_{g=0}^\infty\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{s}^{(g)\prime}P_{t}^{(t-s)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{(t-s)}Q_{s}^{(g)}\right)\right]\\
\leq&c_{PQ}\sum_{s=1}^T\sum_{t=s}^T\mathrm{tr}\left[\sum_{g=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(Q_{s}^{(g)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{t}^{(t-s)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{(t-s)}\right)\right]\\
\leq&c_{PQ}\sum_{s=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left[\sum_{g=0}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(Q_{s}^{(g)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)\sum_{t=s}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(P_{t}^{(t-s)\prime}B_{1t}^{\prime}B_{2t}Q_{t}^{(t-s)}\right)\right]\\
=&O(NT).
\end{align*}
The last term in equation (\ref{eq_Var}) is also $O(NT)$, so $\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t'R_t\right)=O(NT)$.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_ddot}
$\mathbb{L'PR}=\mathbb{L'D(D'D)}^{-1}\mathbb{D'R}=(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h})'(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h})$, where 
\begin{align*}
\ddot{P}_h=\begin{cases} 
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=T-h}^TD_t'P_{t,t+h-T} & \textrm{for } h<T \\
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'P_{t,t+h-T} & \text{for } h\geq T
\end{cases},
\end{align*}
$\ddot{Q}_h$ has the same pattern, and $\mathcal{T}\equiv \mathrm{diag}(T_1,T_2,\cdots,T_N)$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
According to the definitions of $\mathbb{L}$, $\mathbb{R}$ and $\mathbb{D}$, we have
$$\mathbb{D'D}=\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'D_t=\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{diag}(d_{1t},d_{2t},\cdots,d_{N,t})=\mathrm{diag}(T_1,T_2,\cdots,T_N)=\mathcal{T}.$$
Then
$$\mathbb{L'D(D'D)}^{-1}\mathbb{D'R}=\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'L_t\right)'\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'R_t\right).$$
Since
\begin{align*}
L_{1}=&P_{10}V_{1}+P_{11}V_{0}+P_{12}V_{-1}+\cdots,\\
L_{2}=P_{20}V_{2}+&P_{21}V_{1}+P_{22}V_{0}+P_{23}V_{-1}+\cdots,\\
&\vdots\\
L_{T}=P_{T0}V_{T}\cdots+&P_{T,T-1}V_{1}+P_{T,T}V_{0}+P_{T,T+1}V_{-1}+\cdots,
\end{align*}
we have
\begin{align*}
\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'L_{t}=&D_T'P_{T0}V_{T}+(D_{T-1}'P_{T-1,0}+D_{T}'P_{T1})V_{T-1}
%+(D_{T-2}'P_{T-2,0}+D_{T-1}'P_{T-1,1}+D_{T}'P_{T2})V_{T-2}
+\cdots\\
&+(D_1'P_{11}+D_2'P_{22}+\cdots+D_T'P_{TT})V_0+(D_1'P_{12}+D_2'P_{23}+\cdots+D_T'P_{T,T+1})V_{-1}+\cdots.
\end{align*}
Therefore
$$\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'L_t=\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'\sum_{h=0}^\infty P_{th}V_{t-h}=\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h},$$
where
\begin{align*}
\ddot{P}_h=\begin{cases} 
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=T-h}^TD_t'P_{t,t+h-T} & \textrm{for } h<T \\
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'P_{t,t+h-T} & \text{for } h\geq T
\end{cases}.
\end{align*}
Similarly, $\ddot{Q}_h$ has the same pattern.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_PPQ}
Under Assumptions 1, 2, 7 and \ref{asp_A2},
\begin{gather}
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}'\mathbb{R}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}'\mathbb{R}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_lr}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right),
\label{eq_lpr}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L'QR}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L'QR}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_lqr}
\end{gather}
where $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}'\mathbb{R}\right)=O(1)$ and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_lr})}

According to Lemmas \ref{lm_EPQ} and \ref{lm33}, we have $$\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}'\mathbb{R}\right)=\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t'R_t\right)=\frac{1}{NT}\sigma_0^2\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty P_{th}Q_{th}'\right)=O(1)$$
and according to Lemma \ref{lm_OnT}, $\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{L}'\mathbb{R})=\mathrm{Var}(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t'R_t)=O(NT)$. Using Chebyshev's inequality, we can prove equation (\ref{eq_lr}).

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_lpr})}

According to Lemmas \ref{lm_ddot} and \ref{lm_CovPQ}, we have
\begin{align}
\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}\right)=&\mathrm{Cov}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right),\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right)\right]\nonumber\\
=&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right)\right]+\nonumber\\
&\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right],\label{eq_VLPR}
\end{align}
where $\ddot{P}_g$ and $\ddot{Q}_g$ are defined as in Lemma \ref{lm_ddot}. $\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'=\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'+\sum_{g=T}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'$, $p=1$ and $p=\infty$,
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p&=\left\|\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \left(\sum_{t=T-g}^TD_t'Q_{t,t+g-T}\right)\left(\sum_{t=T-g}^TD_t'P_{t,t+g-T}\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_p\\
&\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_p^2\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \left\|\sum_{t=T-g}^TD_t'Q_{t,t+g-T}\right\|_p\left\|\left(\sum_{t=T-g}^TD_t'P_{t,t+g-T}\right)'\right\|_p\\
&\leq\frac{1}{c_T T}\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \left\|\sum_{t=T-g}^TD_t'B_{2t}Q_t^{(t+g-T)}\right\|_p\left\|\left(\sum_{t=T-g}^TD_t'B_{1t}P_t^{(t+g-T)}\right)'\right\|_p\\
&\leq\frac{1}{c_T T}\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \left\|\sum_{t=T-g}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_t'B_{2t}Q_t^{(t+g-T)}\right)\right\|_p\left\|\sum_{t=T-g}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_t'B_{1t}P_t^{(t+g-T)}\right)'\right\|_p\\
&=O(1),
\end{align*}
where the second inequality holds because $T_i\geq c_T T$ in Assumption 1 and the last equality follows from Lemma \ref{lm33}. Likewise, for $p=1$ and $p=\infty$,
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{g=T}^{\infty} \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p&=\left\|\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{g=T}^{\infty} \left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'Q_{t,t+g-T}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'P_{t,t+g-T}\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_p\\
&=\left\|\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{g=T}^{\infty} \left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}Q_t^{(t)}\right)Q_0^{(g-T)}P_0^{(g-T)\prime}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{1t}P_t^{(t)}\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_p\\
&\leq\|\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_p^2\left\|\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}Q_t^{(t)}\right\|_p\left\|\sum_{g=T}^{\infty}Q_0^{(g-T)}P_0^{(g-T)\prime}\right\|_p\left\|\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{1t}P_t^{(t)}\right)'\right\|_p\\
&=O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),
\end{align*}
where the second equality holds because $P_{t,t+g-T}=B_{1t}P_{t}^{(t)}P_{0}^{(g-T)}$ and $Q_{t,t+g-T}=B_{2t}Q_{t}^{(t)}Q_{0}^{(g-T)}$. The last quality holds because of Assumption 1 and Lemma \ref{lm33}. Therefore,
$$\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p\leq\left\|\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p+\left\|\sum_{g=T}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p=O(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)=O(1).$$
Since $P_{th}$ and $Q_{th}$ are arbitrary matrices, under the same assumptions, similarly we have $\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p=O(1)$ and $\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right\|_p=O(1)$ for $p=1$ and $p=\infty$. Then
\begin{align*}
&\left\|\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right)\right\|_p\\
\leq&\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p^2+\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right\|_p\\
=&O(1),
\end{align*}
for $p=1$ and $p=\infty$. So the order of the first term in equation (\ref{eq_VLPR}) can be derived as
$$\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right)\right]=O(N).$$
Furthermore, 
\begin{align*}
\left|\sum_{g=0}^\infty\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right|&\leq\sup_{g,i}\left|\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right|\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left|\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left(D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right)_{ii}\right|\right)\\
&\leq\sup_{g}\left\|D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right\|_1\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right)\right]\\
&=O(N),
\end{align*}
where the last equality holds because both $\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\|_1$ and $\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\|_\infty$ are $O(1)$, so the $\sup$ in the expression is $O(1)$, and the trace is $O(N)$.
Thus, the second term in equation \ref{eq_VLPR} is also $O(N)$. Therefore, $\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR})=O(N)$, then
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right)$$
according to Chebyshev's inequality. Also, according to Lemma \ref{lm_EPQ},
\begin{align*}
\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L'}\mathbb{PR}\right)=\frac{1}{NT}\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_{h}\ddot{Q}_{h}'\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).
\end{align*}


\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_lqr})}

Since $\mathbb{Q}=I_n-\mathbb{P}$, combining equations (\ref{eq_lr}) and (\ref{eq_lpr}), the results follows.

%So $\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'$ is UB which means that $\mathrm{tr}[(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}')]=O(N)$
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_CQL}
Under Assumptions 2, 7, \ref{asp_A2} and \ref{asp_C}, we have

\begin{gather}
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'L}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_cl}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'}\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{L}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_cql}
\end{gather}
\begin{gather}
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_cv}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'}\mathbb{QB}_1\mathbb{V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_cqv}
\end{gather}
where $E(\mathbb{L})=0$ and $E(\mathbb{V})=0$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}

\textbf{Proof of equations (\ref{eq_cl}) and (\ref{eq_cql})}

Since $E(L_t)=0$ and $C_t$, $B_t$, $D_t$ are nonstochastic, $E(\mathbb{L})=E(\mathbb{C'L})=E(\mathbb{C'}\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{L})=0$. Additionally, 
\begin{align*}
\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{C'L}\right)&=\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TC_t'L_t\right)=\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T\mathrm{Cov}\left(C_t'L_t,C_s'L_s\right)\leq\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T\left|C_t'E\left(L_tL_s'\right)C_s\right|\\
&\leq2\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\left|C_t'E\left(L_tL_s'\right)C_s\right|=2\sigma_0^2\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\left|C_t'\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}'\right)C_s\right|,
\end{align*}
where the last equality follows from Lemma \ref{lm_EPQ}. According to Assumption \ref{asp_A2}, $\{B_{1t}\}$ is UB uniformly for $t$ so there exists a constant $c_B>0$ such that
$$\sup_{N,T}\sup_{t\leq T}\max\left(\left\|B_t\right\|_{1},\left\|B_t\right\|_{\infty}\right)\leq c_B.$$
Moreover, by Assumption \ref{asp_C}, elements in $\mathbb{C}$ are uniformly bounded, and the same holds for $\mathbb{QC}$ as $\mathbb{Q}$ is the projection matrix. Thus, there exists a constant $c_C>0$ such that
$$\sup_N\sup_{T}\sup_{1\leq i\leq n}\max\left(\left|(\mathbb{C})_i\right|,\left|(\mathbb{QC})_i\right|\right)\leq c_C.$$
Hence, for any t and $1\leq i\leq N_t$, we have
$$\left|\left(B_{1t}'C_t\right)_i\right|=\left|\sum_{j=1}^{N_t}\left(B_{1t}'\right)_{ij}(C_t)_j\right|\leq c_Bc_C,$$
therefore,
\begin{align*}
\left|C_t'\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{t,t-s+g}P_{sg}'\right)C_s\right|&=\left|C_t'B_{1t}P_t^{(t-s)}\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left(P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)B_{1s}'C_s\right|
\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\left|\left(B_{1t}'C_t\right)_i\left(P_t^{(t-s)}\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)_{ij}\left(B_{1s}'C_s\right)_j\right|\\
&\leq c_B^2c_C^2\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\left|\left(P_t^{(t-s)}\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)_{ij}\right|,
\end{align*}
where the first equation holds by $P_{th}=B_{1t}P_t^{(h)}$ and $P_t^{(t-s+g)}=P_t^{(t-s)}P_s^{(g)}$. So
\begin{align*}
\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{C'L}\right)&\leq2c_B^2c_C^2\sigma_0^2\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\left|\left(P_t^{(t-s)}\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)_{ij}\right|\\
&=2c_B^2c_C^2\sigma_0^2\sum_{s=1}^T\sum_{t=s}^T\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\left|\left(D_t'P_t^{(t-s)}\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)_{ij}\right|\\
&\leq2c_B^2c_C^2\sigma_0^2\sum_{s=1}^T\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}\sum_{j=1}^{N_s}\left|\left[\sum_{t=s}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_t'P_t^{(t-s)}\right)\sum_{g=0}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)\right]_{ij}\right|\\
&=O(NT),
\end{align*}
where the last equality holds because $\sum_{t=s}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(D_t'P_t^{(t-s)}\right)\sum_{g=0}^\infty \mathrm{abs}\left(P_s^{(g)}P_s^{(g)\prime}\right)$ is UB by Assumption \ref{asp_A2} and Lemma \ref{lm33}. Then we can obtain equation (\ref{eq_cl}) by Chebyshev's inequality. By the same reasoning, we can obtain the same order for $\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{C'QL}\right)$ by replacing $\mathbb{C}$ with $\mathbb{QC}$ from the previous analysis. Therefore, equation (\ref{eq_cql}) follows.

\textbf{Proof of equations (\ref{eq_cv}) and (\ref{eq_cqv})}

Since $E(V_t)=0$, $E(\mathbb{C'V})=E(\mathbb{C'QB}_1\mathbb{V})=0$. Because the error terms $v_{it}$ are i.i.d. across $i$ and $t$, we have 
\begin{align*}
\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{C'V})=\mathrm{Var}(\sum_{t=1}^TC_t'V_t)=\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{Var}(C_t'V_t)=\sigma_0^2\sum_{t=1}^TC_t'C_t=O(NT),
\end{align*}
where the last equality holds because elements in $C_t$ are uniformly bounded. Then we can obtain equation (\ref{eq_cv}) by Chebyshev's inequality. Since $\{B_{1t}\}$ is UB uniformly for $t$, elements in $\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{QC}$ are also uniformly bounded, we therefore have $\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{C'QB}_1\mathbb{V}\right)=O(NT)$ by the same reasoning. Therefore, equation (\ref{eq_cqv}) follows.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_Z_gamma}
Under Assumptions 1, 2, 7, \ref{asp_A2}, we have
\begin{align}
\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{L}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZgBL}\\
\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{QL}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZgBQL}
\end{align}
and
\begin{align}
\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZgBV}\\
\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{QB}_1\mathbb{V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right).\label{eq_ZgBQV}
\end{align}
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_ZgBL}) and (\ref{eq_ZgBQL})}

As $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}=((D_1F_1\iota_N)',\cdots,(D_TF_T\iota_N)')'$, we have
\begin{align*}
&\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{L}\right)=\mathrm{Var}\left[\sum_{t=1}^T(D_tF_t\iota_N)'B_{2t}'L_t\right]\\
\leq&2\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left[(D_tF_t\iota_N)'B_{2t}'L_t,(D_sF_s\iota_N)'B_{2s}'L_s\right]\right|\\
\leq&2\iota_N'\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^t\mathrm{abs}\left[F_t'D_t'B_{2t}'E\left(L_t L_s^{\prime}\right)B_{2s}D_sF_s\right]\iota_N\\
=&2\sigma_0^2\iota_N'\sum_{s=1}^T\sum_{t=s}^T\mathrm{abs}\left[F_t'D_t'B_{2t}'B_{1t} \left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{t}^{(t-s+g)}P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)B_{1s}^{\prime}
B_{2s}D_sF_s\right]\iota_N\\
\leq&2\sigma_0^2\iota_N'\sum_{s=1}^T\sum_{t=s}^T\mathrm{abs}\left(F_t'D_t'B_{2t}'B_{1t}P_{t}^{(t-s)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{s}^{(g)}P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)B_{1s}^{\prime}
B_{2s}D_s\right]F_s\iota_N\\
\leq&2\sigma_0^2\sum_{s=1}^T\left\{\iota_N'\left[\sum_{t=s}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(D_t'B_{2t}'B_{1t}P_{t}^{(t-s)}\right)\right]\mathrm{abs}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{s}^{(g)}P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)B_{1s}^{\prime}
B_{2s}D_s\right]\right\}F_s\iota_N\\
=&O(N),
\end{align*}
where the second equality follows from Lemma \ref{lm_EPQ}, and the third inequality is due to $P_{t}^{(t-s+g)}=P_{t}^{(t-s)}P_{s}^{(g)}$. 
The final inequality holds because $F_t$ is a diagonal matrix with diagonal entries equal to either 0 or 1. Furthermore, the last equality holds because $\sum_{t=s}^\infty\mathrm{abs}\left(D_t'B_{2t}'B_{1t}P_{t}^{(t-s)}\right)$ and $\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty P_{s}^{(g)}P_{s}^{(g)\prime}\right)$ are UB by Lemma \ref{lm33}, then each element of the vector inside the braces is $O(1)$. Therefore, the entire expression is $O(N)$ because $F_s\iota_N$ is a sparse binary vector with fewer than $N$ nonzero entries in total over $s =1,\cdots,T$. Due to $E(\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{L})=0$, by Chebyshev's inequality we have $\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{L}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$. Additionally,
\begin{align*}
&\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PL})\\
=&\mathrm{Var}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}D_tF_t\iota_N\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{1t}\sum_{h=0}^\infty P_{t}^{(h)}V_{t-h}\right)\right]\\
=&\mathrm{Var}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}D_tF_t\iota_N\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{h}V_{T-h}\right)\right]\\
=&\sigma_0^2\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}D_tF_t\iota_N\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_{h}\ddot{P}_{h}'\right)\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}D_tF_t\iota_N\right)\\
\leq&\frac{\sigma_0^2}{c_TT}\left(\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\sum_{t=1}^TF_t\iota_N\right)'\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_{h}\ddot{P}_{h}'\right)\left(\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\sum_{t=1}^TF_t\iota_N\right)\\
\leq&\sigma_0^2\|\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\|_\infty^2\left[\iota_N'\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_{h}\ddot{P}_{h}'\right)\iota_N\right]\\
=&O\left(\frac{N}{T}\right),
\end{align*}
where $\ddot{P}_h$ is defined in Lemma \ref{lm_ddot}. The first inequality holds because $T_i\geq c_TT$ by Assumption 1. The second inequality above holds due to the sparsity of $F_t\iota_N$, specifically, for each $i$, $(F_{t})_{ii}=d_{it}(1-d_{i,t-1})$ equals 1 at most once over $t = 1, \cdots, T$ (indicating the first appearance of the $i$-th individual) and is zero for other $t$. Therefore, for $i=1,\cdots,N$, we have
$$\left[\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\sum_{t=1}^TF_t\iota_N\right]_i\leq\left[\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\iota_N\right]_i\leq\|\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}D_t)\|_\infty.$$
In addition, the last equality holds because, similar to the corresponding term in equation (\ref{eq_VLPR}) in the proof of Lemma \ref{lm_PPQ}, $\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_{h}\ddot{P}_{h}'\right)$ is UB, then the value inside the brackets is $O(N)$. Hence, we have $\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PL}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right)$ by Chebyshev's inequality. Combining with equation (\ref{eq_ZgBL}), we can prove (\ref{eq_ZgBQL}).

\textbf{Proof of equations (\ref{eq_ZgBV}) and (\ref{eq_ZgBQV})}

Since $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}=\left((D_1F_1\iota_N)',\cdots,(D_TF_T\iota_N)'\right)'$ is a sparse binary vector with at most $N$ nonzero elements and $\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{B}_2$ is UB, $\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{V})=\sigma_0^2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}=O(N)$. Then $\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$ by Chebyshev's inequality. Moreover, since $D_t$ is an $N_t\times N$ matrix obtained by removing the corresponding rows of unobserved nodes during period $t$ from $I_N$, we have
\begin{align*}
\|\mathbb{P}\|_{1}=\|\mathbb{P}\|_{\infty}&=\|\mathrm{abs}\left(\mathbb{D}\mathcal{T}^{-1}\mathbb{D}'\right)\iota_n\|_\infty=\|(D_1',\cdots,D_T')'\mathcal{T}^{-1}(D_1',\cdots,D_T')\iota_n\|_\infty\\
&=\|(D_1',\cdots,D_T')'\mathcal{T}^{-1}(T_1,\cdots,T_N)'\|_\infty=(D_1',\cdots,D_T')'\iota_N=\|\iota_n\|_\infty=1,
\end{align*}
where the third equality holds because all entries of the matrices $\{D_t\}$ and $\mathcal{T}^{-1}$ are all nonnegative. The above means that $\mathbb{P}$ is also UB. Thus,
$\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{V})=\sigma_0^2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PB}_1\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}=O(N)$
because $\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PB}_1\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{PB}_2$ is UB. By Chebyshev's inequality, we also have $\frac{\sqrt{T}}{NT}\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PB}_1\mathbb{V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$, then equation (\ref{eq_ZgBQV}) follows.

\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_lag}
Lemmas \ref{lm_PPQ}-\ref{lm_Z_gamma} still hold by replacing $\mathbb{L}$ and $\mathbb{R}$ with $\mathbb{L}^\ell$ and $\mathbb{R}^\ell$ respectively.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
By definition, we have $L_t^\ell=\sum_{h=0}^\infty B_{1t}^\ell B_{1,t-1}P_{t-1}^{(h)}V_{t-h-1}$ and $R_t^\ell=\sum_{h=0}^\infty B_{2t}^\ell B_{2,t-1}Q_{t-1}^{(h)}V_{t-h-1}$, where $\|\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{1t}^\ell B_{1,t-1}D_{t-1})\|_\infty<\infty$ and $\|\sup_t\mathrm{abs}(D_t'B_{2t}^\ell B_{2,t-1}D_{t-1})\|_\infty<\infty$ according to Lemma \ref{lm33}. Moreover, since $v_{it}$'s are i.i.d. with respect to $i$ and $t$, $\mathbb{L}^\ell$ and $\mathbb{R}^\ell$ also satisfy the conditions of Lemmas \ref{lm_PPQ}-\ref{lm_Z_gamma}, leading to the same result.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_LQV}
Under Assumptions 1, 2, 7 and \ref{asp_A2},
\begin{gather}
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_lbv}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right),
\label{eq_lpv}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_lqv}
\end{gather}
where 
\begin{align}
E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=0,\label{eq_ELV}
\end{align} and
\begin{align}
E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathcal{T}^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\sum_{t=T-h}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right]=O(N).\label{eq_ELPV}
\end{align}
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_lbv})}

For simplicity, let $B_t^\ell\equiv B_{1t}^\ell B_{1,t-1}$.
Since $v_{it}$ are i.i.d across $i$ and $t$,
$$E(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V})=E\left(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_t\right)=\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{h=0}^\infty E\left( V_{t-h-1}'P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_t\right)=0.$$
Then
\begin{align*}
\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V})&=\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TL_t^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_t\right)=\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^TE\left(L_t^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_tL_s^{\ell\prime}B_{2s}V_s\right)\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T\sum_{h=0}^\infty\sum_{g=0}^\infty E\left(V_{t-h-1}'P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_tV_{s-g-1}'P_{s-1}^{(g)\prime}B_{s}^{\ell\prime}B_{2s}V_s\right)\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{h=0}^\infty E\left(V_{t-h-1}'P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_tV_{t-h-1}'P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}V_t\right)\\
&=\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{h=0}^\infty\mathrm{tr}\left(P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}B_{2t}'B_{t}^{\ell}P_{t-1}^{(h)}\right)\\
&=\sigma_0^4\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left(B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}B_{2t}'B_{t}^{\ell}\sum_{h=0}^\infty P_{t-1}^{(h)}P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}\right)\\
&=O(NT),
\end{align*}
where the fourth equality holds because the expectation is nonzero only when $t=s$ and $h=g$ according to Lemma \ref{lm_quadratic form}, and the last equality holds because $B_{t}^{\ell\prime}B_{2t}B_{2t}'B_{t}^{\ell}$ and $\sum_{h=0}^\infty P_{t-1}^{(h)}P_{t-1}^{(h)\prime}$ are UB uniformly for $t$ by Assumption \ref{asp_A2} and Lemma \ref{lm33}. Therefore, we can prove equation (\ref{eq_lbv}) by Chebyshev's inequality.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_lpv})}

Similar to Lemma \ref{lm_ddot}, we have
$$\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_tL_t^{\ell}\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}V_t\right)\equiv\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right),$$
where
\begin{align*}
\ddot{P}_h\equiv\begin{cases}
0 & \textrm{for } h=0 \\
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=T-h}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)} & \textrm{for } 0<h<T \\
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)} & \text{for } h\geq T
\end{cases}\textrm{ and }
\ddot{Q}_h\equiv\begin{cases} 
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{T-h}'B_{2,T-h} & \textrm{for } h<T \\
0 & \text{for } h\geq T
\end{cases}.
\end{align*}
Therefore, by Lemma \ref{lm_EPQ},
\begin{align*}
&\left|E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_h\ddot{Q}_h'\right)\right|\\
=&\left|\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right]\right|\\
%\\
\leq&\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathcal{T}^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right)\right]\\
\leq&\frac{\sigma_0^2}{c_TT}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right)\right]\\
=&O(N),
\end{align*}
%$\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{i=1}^N \frac{A_{t,ii}}{T_i}=O(N)$
%where 
%$$E_R\equiv\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathcal{T}^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\left(\sum_{t=T}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right].$$
%Hence,
%\begin{align*}
%&\left|\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathcal{T}^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right]\right|\\
%\leq&\frac{\sigma_0^2}{c_TT}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right)\right]\\
%=&O(N),
%\end{align*}
where the second inequality holds because $T_i>c_TT$ by Assumption 1 and the last equality holds because the matrix under the trace is UB uniformly for $h$ according to Lemma \ref{lm33}.
%And
%\begin{align*}
%&\left|\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathcal{T}^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\left(\sum_{t=T}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right]\right|\\
%=&\left|\sigma_0^2\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathcal{T}^{-1}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\sum_{t=T}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t-T)}P_{T}^{(h)}B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right]\right|\\
%\leq&\frac{\sigma_0^2}{c_TT}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t-T)}\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}P_{T}^{(h)}B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}\right)\right]\\
%=&O\left(\frac{N}{T}\right),
%\end{align*}
%where the inequality holds because $T_i>c_TT$ by Assumption 1 and the last equality holds because $\sum_{t=T-h}^{\infty}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t-T)}$ and $\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}P_{T}^{(h)}B_{2,T-h}'D_{T-h}$ are UB according to Lemma \ref{lm33}. Therefore, $E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(N)+O\left(\frac{N}{T}\right)$.
By Lemma \ref{lm_CovPQ}, we have
\begin{align}
\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=&\mathrm{Cov}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right),\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right)\right]\nonumber\\
=&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right)\right]+\nonumber\\
&\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right],\label{eq_Varlpv}
\end{align}
where
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p=&\left\|
\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{T-h}'B_{2,T-h}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^{T-1}D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_p\\
\leq&\frac{1}{c_TT}\sum_{h=1}^{T-1}\left\|D_{T-h}'B_{2,T-h}\left(\sum_{t=T-h}^\infty D_{t+1}'B_{t+1}^\ell P_{t}^{(t+h-T)}\right)'\right\|_p\\
=&O(1)
\end{align*}
holds for $p=1$ and $p=\infty$.
Since the $\ddot{P}_h$ here can be regarded as a lagged version of that defined in Lemma \ref{lm_ddot}, and $\|\sup_t \mathrm{abs}(B_t^\ell)\|_\infty<\infty$ by Lemma \ref{lm33}, similar to the derivation of the upper bound for the corresponding term in equation (\ref{eq_VLPR}), we have
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right\|_p=O(1)
\end{align*}
for $p=1$ and $p=\infty$. Additionally,
\begin{align*}
\left\|\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right\|_p&=\left\|\sum_{g=0}^{T-1} \mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{T-g}'B_{2,T-g}B_{2,T-g}'D_{T-g}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_p\\
&\leq\frac{1}{c_TT}\sum_{g=0}^{T-1}\left\|D_{T-g}'B_{2,T-g}B_{2,T-g}'D_{T-g}\right\|_p\\
&=\frac{1}{c_TT}\sum_{g=0}^{T-1}\left\|B_{2,T-g}B_{2,T-g}'\right\|_p\\
&=O(1),
\end{align*}
where the first inequality holds by $T_i\geq c_TT$ in Assumption 1, the second one holds because $D_{T-g}'B_{2,T-g}B_{2,T-g}'D_{T-g}$ is merely an augmented version of $B_{2,T-g}B_{2,T-g}'$ with additional all-zero rows and columns, and the last equality holds because $B_{2,T-g}B_{2,T-g}'$ is UB uniformly for $g$. Therefore, the matrix within the trace in the first term of equation (\ref{eq_Varlpv}) is UB, which means that the first term is $O(N)$. For the second term in equation (\ref{eq_Varlpv}), we have
\begin{align*}
\left|\sum_{g=0}^\infty\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right|&\leq\sup_{g,i}\left|\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right|\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left|\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left(D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right)_{ii}\right|\right)\\
&\leq\sup_{g}\left\|D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right\|_1\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right)\right]\\
&=O(N),
\end{align*}
where the last equality holds because $\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}$ is UB, so $\sup_{g}\left\|D_g'\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}D_g\right\|_1$ is $O(1)$, and the trace above is $O(N)$. So the second term in equation (\ref{eq_Varlpv}) is also $O(N)$, which means that the whole term is $O(N)$. Therefore, we can get equation (\ref{eq_lpv}) by Chebyshev's inequality.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_lqv})}

Since $\mathbb{Q}=I_n-\mathbb{P}$, combining equations (\ref{eq_lbv}) and (\ref{eq_lpv}), the results follows.
\end{proof}

%Denote $\mathbb{B}_1^\ell=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{11}^\ell,B_{12}^\ell,\cdots,B_{1T}^\ell)$, $\mathbb{B}_2^\ell=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{21}^\ell,B_{22}^\ell,\cdots,B_{2T}^\ell)$, $\mathbb{L}_{-1}=(L_0',L_1',$ $\cdots,L_{T-1}')'$ and $\mathbb{R}_{-1}=(R_0',R_1',\cdots,R_{T-1}')'$.
%$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}_{-1}'\mathbb{R}_{-1}-\frac{1}{NT}E(\mathbb{L}_{-1}'\mathbb{R}_{-1})=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}})$$
%$$\mathbb{L}^{\ell'}\mathbb{R}^\ell=.$$
%$$\mathbb{L}^{\ell'}\mathbb{QR}^\ell=\mathbb{L}_{-1}'\mathbb{B}_1^{\ell'}\mathbb{QB}_2^\ell\mathbb{R}_{-1}.$$

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_VQV}
Under Assumptions
1, 2, 7 and \ref{asp_A2},
\begin{gather}
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{VB}'_1\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_vbv}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right),
\label{eq_vpv}\\
\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_vqv}
\end{gather}
where $\frac{1}{NT}E(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V})=O(1)$ and $\frac{1}{NT}E(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V})=O(\frac{1}{T})$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_vbv})}
\begin{align*}
\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)&=\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\sum_{t=1}^T V_t'B_{1t}'B_{2t}V_t\right)=\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\sum_{t=1}^T \mathrm{tr}(B_{2t}V_tV_t'B_{1t}')\right]\\
&=\frac{1}{NT}\sigma^2_0\sum_{t=1}^T \mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})=O(1),
\end{align*}
where the last equality is because $\{B_{1t}\}$ and $\{B_{2t}\}$ are UB uniform for $t$.
Moreover by Lemma \ref{lm_quadratic form},
\begin{align*}
&\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)\\
=&\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T\mathrm{Cov}(V_t'B_{1t}'B_{2t}V_t,V_s'B_{1s}'B_{2s}V_s)\\
=&\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T \left[E(V_t'B_{1t}'B_{2t}V_tV_s'B_{1s}'B_{2s}V_s)-E(V_t'B_{1t}'B_{2t}V_t)E(V_s'B_{1s}'B_{2s}V_s)\right]\\
=&\sum_{t=1}^T\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma^4_0)\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})_{ii}^2+\sigma_0^4\left(\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})^2+\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t}B_{1t}'B_{2t})+\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t}B_{2t}'B_{1t})\right)\right]+\\
&\sum_{t=1}^T\sum_{s=1}^T\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})\mathrm{tr}(B_{1s}'B_{2s})-\sum_{t=1}^T\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})^2-\sigma_0^4\left[\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})\right]^2\\
=&\sum_{t=1}^T\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma^4_0)\sum_{i=1}^{N_t}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})_{ii}^2+\sigma_0^4\left(\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t}B_{1t}'B_{2t})+\mathrm{tr}(B_{1t}'B_{2t}B_{2t}'B_{1t})\right)\right]\\
=&O(NT).
\end{align*}
Then we can get equation (\ref{eq_vbv}) by Chebyshev's inequality.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_vpv})}

Similar to Lemma \ref{lm_ddot}, we have
\begin{align*}
\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{1t}V_t\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}V_t\right)\equiv\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right),
\end{align*}
where
\begin{align*}
\ddot{P}_h\equiv\begin{cases} 
\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{T-h}'B_{1,T-h} & \textrm{for } h<T \\
0 & \text{for } h\geq T
\end{cases}
\end{align*}
and $\ddot{Q}_h$ has the same pattern. Then according to Lemma \ref{lm_EPQ},
\begin{align*}
&\left|\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)\right|=\frac{1}{NT}\left|E\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right)\right]\right|\\
=&\frac{1}{NT}\sigma_0^2\left|\mathrm{tr}\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_{h}\ddot{Q}_{h}'\right)\right|\leq\frac{1}{NT}\sigma_0^2\sum_{t=1}^T\left|\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{t}'B_{1t}
B_{1t}'D_t\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\frac{1}{c_TNT^2}\sigma_0^2\sum_{t=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{abs}(B_{1t}'B_{2t})\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right),
\end{align*}
The second equality holds because $V_t$'s are independent across $t$. The second inequality holds due to Assumption 1 with $T_i\geq c_TT$. The last equality holds because $\{B_{1t}\}$ and $\{B_{2t}\}$ are UB uniform for $t$.
Moreover according to Lemma \ref{lm_CovPQ},
\begin{align}
&\mathrm{Var}\left(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)\nonumber\\
=&\mathrm{Cov}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right),\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{P}_hV_{T-h}\right)'\left(\sum_{h=0}^\infty\ddot{Q}_hV_{T-h}\right)\right]\nonumber\\
=&\sigma_0^4\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)+\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{P}_{g}\ddot{P}_{g}'\right)\left(\sum_{g=0}^\infty \ddot{Q}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}'\right)\right]+\nonumber\\
&\sum_{g=0}^\infty\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{T-g}}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\left(\ddot{P}_{g}'\ddot{Q}_{g}\right)_{ii}\right]\nonumber\\
=&\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=1}^T\sum_{h=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{g}'B_{2g}B_{1g}'D_{g}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left( \mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{h}'B_{2h}B_{1h}'D_{h}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]+\nonumber\\
&\sigma_0^4\sum_{g=1}^T\sum_{h=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{g}'B_{1g}B_{1g}'D_{g}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{h}'B_{2h}B_{2h}'D_{h}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]+\nonumber\\
&\sum_{g=1}^T\left[(\mu_4-3\sigma_0^4)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{g}}\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)_{ii}\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)_{ii}\right].\label{eq_Vvpv}
\end{align}
Since for any $g$ and $h$,
\begin{align*}
&\left|\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{g}'B_{2g}B_{1g}'D_{g}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{h}'B_{2h}B_{1h}'D_{h}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]\right|\\
=&\left|\mathrm{tr}\left[D_{h}\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_{g}'B_{2g}B_{1g}'D_{g}\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_{h}'B_{2h}B_{1h}'\right]\right|\\
\leq&\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(D_{h}\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_{g}'\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2g}B_{1g}'\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(D_{g}\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_{h}'\right)\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2h}B_{1h}'\right)\right]\\
\leq&\frac{1}{c_T^2T^2}\mathrm{tr}\left[D_{h}D_{g}'\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2g}B_{1g}'\right)D_{g}D_{h}'\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{2h}B_{1h}'\right)\right]\\
=&O\left(\frac{N}{T^2}\right),
\end{align*}
where the second inequality holds due to Assumption 1 with $T_i\geq c_TT$, and the last equality holds because $\{B_{1t}\}$ and $\{B_{2t}\}$ are UB uniform for $t$. Similarly, we have
$$\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{g}'B_{1g}B_{1g}'D_{g}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left(\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D_{h}'B_{2h}B_{2h}'D_{h}\mathcal{T}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]=O\left(\frac{N}{T^2}\right).$$
Therefore, the first two terms in equation (\ref{eq_Vvpv}) is $O(N)$.
Then, by the same argument, we have
\begin{align*}
\sup_{g,i}\left|\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)_{ii}\right|&\leq\sup_{g,i}\sum_{k=1}^N \mathrm{abs}(B_{1g}'D_g)_{ik}T_k^{-1}\mathrm{abs}(D_g'B_{2g})_{ki}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{c_TT}\sup_{g,i}\sum_{k=1}^N \mathrm{abs}(B_{1g}'D_g)_{ik}\mathrm{abs}(D_g'B_{2g})_{ki}=O\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).   
\end{align*}
Thus,
\begin{align*}
&\left|\sum_{g=1}^T\sum_{i=1}^{N_{g}}\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)_{ii}\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)_{ii}\right|\\
\leq&\sup_{i,g}\left|\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)_{ii}\right|\sum_{g=1}^T\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathrm{abs}\left(B_{1g}'D_g\mathcal{T}^{-1}D_g'B_{2g}\right)\right]\\
=&O\left(\frac{N}{T}\right),
\end{align*}
that is, the last term in equation (\ref{eq_Vvpv}) is $O\left(\frac{N}{T}\right)$. Therefore, $\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{V'B}'_1\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V})=O(N)+O\left(\frac{N}{T}\right)=O(N).$
Then by Chebyshev's inequality, equation (\ref{eq_vpv}) follows.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_vqv})}

Since $\mathbb{Q}=I_n-\mathbb{P}$, combining equations (\ref{eq_vbv}) and (\ref{eq_vpv}), the results follows.
\end{proof}


\begin{lemma}\label{lm15}
Under Assumptions 1-7 and \ref{asp_A2}, we have
%For any series nonstochastic UB and block diagonal matrix $B_{1t}$ and $B_{2t}$ of size $N_t\times N_t$, denote $\mathbb{B}_1=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{1t})$ and $\mathbb{B}_2=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{2t})$ respectively, then we have
\begin{align}
&\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{V}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{V}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_VBV}\\
&\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZBV}\\
&\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}-\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZBZ}
\end{align}
where $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{V}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O(1)$, $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O(\frac{1}{T})$ and $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}]=O(1)$. 
%The results above remain valid when $\mathbb{Z}$ is replaced by $\mathbb{Z}\widetilde{\Gamma}$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_VBV})}

This result is just equation (\ref{eq_vqv}) in Lemma \ref{lm_VQV}.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_ZBV})}

Recall that $Z_t=(D_t(I_N-F_t)D_t'M_tY_{t-1},D_tD_{t-1}'Y_{t-1},D_tF_t\iota_N,X_t)$ and $\mathbb{Z}=(Z_1',Z_2',\cdots,Z_T')'$. Denote $\mathbb{Z}_1$, $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}$ as the first three columns of $\mathbb{Z}$, and let $\mathbb{Z}_4$ denote the remaining $k$ columns.
Since $$\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}=\left(\begin{array}{c}
\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\\
\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\\
\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\\
\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}
\end{array}\right),$$
we only need to derive the quantitative law of large numbers for each element of the above vectors. According to equation (3),
\begin{align*}
Y_t=\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}S_{t}^{-1}A_t^{(h)}[D_{t-h} (\gamma_0F_{t-h}\iota_N+\alpha_0)+X_{t-h}\beta_0+V_{t-h}]=\mathcal{E}_t+\mathcal{X}_t\beta_0+\mathcal{V}_t,
\end{align*}
where $\mathcal{E}_t= \sum_{h=0}^{\infty}S_{t}^{-1}A_t^{(h)}D_{t-h}(\gamma_0F_{t-h}\iota_N+\alpha_0)$, 
%$\mathcal{E}_\alpha\equiv \sum_{h=0}^{\infty}A_t^{(h)}S_{t-h}^{-1}D_{t-h}\alpha_0$, 
$\mathcal{X}_t= \sum_{h=0}^{\infty}S_{t}^{-1}A_t^{(h)}X_{t-h}$ and $\mathcal{V}_t= \sum_{h=0}^{\infty}S_{t}^{-1}A_t^{(h)}V_{t-h}$. Therefore, $\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{Z}_1$ is the stack version of vector $B_{1t}D_t(I_N-F_t)D_t'M_t(\mathcal{E}_{t-1}+\mathcal{X}_{t-1}\beta_0+\mathcal{V}_{t-1})$, which can be viewed as $\mathbb{C+L}^\ell$, by specifying $C_t=B_{1t}D_t(I_N-F_t)D_t'M_t(\mathcal{E}_{t-1}+\mathcal{X}_{t-1}\beta_0)$ and $L_{t}^\ell=B_{1t}D_t(I_N-F_t)D_t'M_t\mathcal{V}_{t-1}$.
%, where $B_{1t}^\ell=D_t(I_N-F_t)D_t'M_t$, $B_{1,t-1}=S_{t-1}^{-1}$ and $P_{t-1}^{(h)}=A_{t-1}^{(h)}$. 
Then we have $$\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\equiv\mathbb{C}'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}+\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V},$$ 
where $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C}'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}})$ by Lemma \ref{lm_CQL}, $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}})$, and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V}\right]=O(\frac{1}{T})$ by Lemma \ref{lm_LQV}. Therefore, 
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$.
Similarly, $\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{Z}_2$ can also be viewed as $\mathbb{C+L}^\ell$ by specifying $C_t=B_{1t}D_tD_{t-1}(\mathcal{E}_{t-1}+\mathcal{X}_{t-1}\beta_0)$ and $L_{t}^\ell=B_{1t}D_tD_{t-1}\mathcal{V}_{t-1}$. Hence,
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$ also hold. Each column of $\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}$ and $\mathbb{B}_1\mathbb{Z}_4$ can be viewed as $\mathbb{C}$ because they are both vectors whose elements are uniformly bounded.
Then $\mathbb{Z}_3$ and $\mathbb{Z}_4$ satisfy the same conclusion because $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C}'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}})$ by Lemma \ref{lm_CQL}.
Thus, equation (\ref{eq_ZBV}) holds and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$.
%\mathrm{Var}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{1t}D_tF_t\iota_N\right)'\mathcal{T}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t=1}^TD_t'B_{2t}V_t\right)\right]
%$E(\mathbb{Z}_3'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{QB}_2\mathbb{V})=0$
%\mathrm{Var}\left[\sum_{t=1}^T(D_tF_t\iota_N)'B_{1t}'B_{2t}V_t\right]

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_ZBZ})}

We have 
\begin{align*}
\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}=\left(\begin{array}{cccc}
\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\\
\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\\
\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\\
\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4
\end{array}\right).
\end{align*}
Firstly, we can decompose $$\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1\equiv(\mathbb{C}_1+\mathbb{L}^\ell)'\mathbb{Q(C}_2+\mathbb{R}^\ell)=\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{QC}_2+\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{QR}^\ell+\mathbb{C}_2'\mathbb{QL}^\ell+\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QR}^\ell,$$
where $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'}_1\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{R}^\ell=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$ and $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'}_2\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{L}^\ell=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$ by Lemmas \ref{lm_CQL} and \ref{lm_lag}, and $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QR}^\ell-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QR}^\ell\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$, $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{QR}^\ell\right)=O(1)$ by Lemmas \ref{lm_PPQ} and \ref{lm_lag}. We have $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{QC}_2=O(1)$ because elements in $\mathbb{C}_1$ and $\mathbb{C}_2$ are uniformly bounded and $\mathbb{Q}$ is a projection matrix. Hence,
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1\right)=O(1)$.
The same conclusion holds for $\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2$, $\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2$ as they can undergo similar decomposition.

Secondly, we have $\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}\equiv(\mathbb{C}_1+\mathbb{L}^\ell)'\mathbb{QC}_2=\mathbb{C}_2'\mathbb{QC}_1+\mathbb{C}_2'\mathbb{QL}^\ell$. Similarly, we have
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}\right)=O(1)$ by Lemmas \ref{lm_CQL} and \ref{lm_lag}. The same conclusion holds for $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1$, $\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$, $\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1$, $\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}$, $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2$, $\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$ and $\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2$.

Lastly, 
The scalar $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}$, as well as each element of the vector $\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$, $\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}$, and the matrix $\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$, can be regarded as $\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{QC}_2$ and $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{QC}_2=O(1)$.
Therefore, equation (\ref{eq_ZBZ}) follows and $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{Q}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}]=O(1)$.

\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{lm_ZPZ}
Under Assumptions 1-7 and \ref{asp_A2}, we have
%For any series nonstochastic UB and block diagonal matrix $B_{1t}$ and $B_{2t}$ of size $N_t\times N_t$, denote $\mathbb{B}_1=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{1t})$ and $\mathbb{B}_2=\mathrm{blkdiag}(B_{2t})$ respectively, then we have
\begin{align}
&\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{V}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{V}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right),\label{eq_VPV}\\
&\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZPV}\\
&\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}-\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\label{eq_ZPZ}
\end{align}
where $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{V}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O(\frac{1}{T})$, $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}]=O(\frac{1}{T})$ and $\frac{1}{NT}E[\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}]=O(1)$. %The results above remain valid when $\mathbb{Z}$ is replaced by $\mathbb{Z}\widetilde{\Gamma}$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_VPV})}

This result is just equation (\ref{eq_vpv}) in Lemma \ref{lm_VQV}.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_ZPV})}

Similar to the proof of Lemma \ref{lm15}, we have
$$\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}=\left(\begin{array}{c}
\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\\
\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\\
\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\\
\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}
\end{array}\right),$$
where $\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\equiv\mathbb{C}'\mathbb{V}+\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}$. We have $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C}'\mathbb{V}=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}})$ by Lemmas \ref{lm_CQL} and \ref{lm_lag}. According to Lemma \ref{lm_LQV}, we have $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}})$  and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PB}_2\mathbb{V}\right]=O(\frac{1}{T})$. Therefore, 
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$.
Similarly,
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
holds and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$.


$\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PB}_1\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)}$ and each column of $\mathbb{B}_2'\mathbb{PB}_1\mathbb{Z}_4$ can be viewed as $\mathbb{C}$ because elements of all these vectors are uniformly bounded.
Then $\mathbb{Z}_3$ and $\mathbb{Z}_4$ satisfy the same conclusion because $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C}'\mathbb{V}=O_p(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}})$ by Lemma \ref{lm_CQL}.
Thus, equation (\ref{eq_ZPV}) holds and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{V}\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$.

\textbf{Proof of equation (\ref{eq_ZPZ})}

We have 
\begin{align*}
\mathbb{Z}'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}=\left(\begin{array}{cccc}
\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\\
\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\\
\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)\prime}\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\\
\mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1 & \mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2 & \mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}^{(\gamma)} & \mathbb{Z}_4'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_4
\end{array}\right).
\end{align*}
Similar to the proof of Lemma \ref{lm15}, we can first decompose $\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1\equiv(\mathbb{C}_1+\mathbb{L}^\ell)'\mathbb{P(C}_2+\mathbb{R}^\ell)=\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{PC}_2+\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{PR}^\ell+\mathbb{C}_2'\mathbb{PL}^\ell+\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PR}^\ell$, where $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'}_1\mathbb{P}\mathbb{R}^\ell=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$ and $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{C'}_2\mathbb{P}\mathbb{L}^\ell=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$ by Lemmas \ref{lm_CQL} and \ref{lm_lag}, and $\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PR}^\ell-\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PR}^\ell\right)=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT^2}}\right)$, $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{L}^{\ell\prime}\mathbb{PR}^\ell\right)=O(\frac{1}{T})$ by Lemmas \ref{lm_PPQ} and \ref{lm_lag}. Moreover, 
$\frac{1}{NT}\left|\mathbb{C}_1'\mathbb{PC}_2\right|=O(1)$
because elements in $\mathbb{C}$ are uniformly bounded and $\mathbb{P}$ is a projection matrix. Hence,
$$\frac{1}{NT}\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1-\frac{1}{NT}E\left[\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1\right]=O_p\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)$$
and $\frac{1}{NT}E\left(\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1\right)=O(1)$.
The same conclusion holds for $\mathbb{Z}_1'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2$, $\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_1$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2'\mathbb{B}_1'\mathbb{P}\mathbb{B}_2\mathbb{Z}_2$ as they can undergo similar decomposition.
